# Skills · E · part 106

820 entries, experiment-design-checklist to experimental-deliberation. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **experiment-design-checklist**: Generates a rigorous experiment design given a hypothesis. Use when asked to design experiments, plan experiments, create an experimental setup, or figure out how to test a research hypothesis. Covers controls, baselines, ablations, metrics, statistical tests, and compute estimates. ([GhostScientist/skills](https://github.com/GhostScientist/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/experiment-design-checklist) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **experiment-design-desk**: design product experiments with hypotheses, metrics, cohorts, variants, guardrails, duration, analysis rules, ethical constraints, and decision criteria. ([MadewellRD/skills-lab](https://github.com/MadewellRD/skills-lab/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/product-command-desk/experiment-design-desk) · ★2 · 4 copies)
- **experiment-design-for-sciml**: Use when designing experiments, baselines, ablations, generalization tests, and physical diagnostics for CFD-AI and scientific ML manuscripts. ([VortexyAether/cfd-ai-paper-skills](https://github.com/VortexyAether/cfd-ai-paper-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/experiment-design-for-sciml) · ★4)
- **experiment-design-framework**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "design an A/B test for ads", "calculate sample size for an experiment", or mentions "statistical significance", "split test", or "test duration". Do NOT use for: incrementality, geo lift or holdout tests (use incrementality-testing-guide), creative fatigue analysis (use meta-creative-fatigue-analyzer), general campaign performance review (use meta-performance-troubleshooter or google-ads-performance-troubleshooter), or audience strategy (use buyer-persona-framework). ([Ad-Superpowers/ad-superpowers-plugin](https://github.com/Ad-Superpowers/ad-superpowers-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/experiment-design-framework) · ★6)
- **experiment-design-helper**: 实验设计引导工作流，覆盖问题定义、设计选择、样本量计算、方案生成四个步骤。 当用户提到：实验设计、样本量估算、研究方案、RCT、随机对照试验、功效分析、 效应量、检验功效、实验方案、研究设计时使用。适用于临床研究、基础科研和量化研究设计阶段。 ([lewisoepwqi/scientific_nini](https://github.com/lewisoepwqi/scientific_nini/tree/HEAD/.nini/skills/experiment-design-helper) · ★2)

- **experiment-design-kit**: Toolkit for structuring hypotheses, variants, guardrails, and measurement. Use when working with experiment design kit. ([REVREBEL/skills-library](https://github.com/REVREBEL/skills-library/tree/HEAD/library/marketing-and-seo/on-page-seo/experiment-design-kit))
- **experiment-design-matrix**: Use when planning experiments, ablations, or evaluation sweeps — before a costly run series, or when adding new cells (e.g. ablations) to a running campaign. Turns pre-registered hypotheses into a factor matrix with fair baselines, metrics, seeds, and stopping criteria. Not needed for trivial probes and smoke tests. - **experiment-design-pipeline**: Run an end-to-end workflow that chains the skills `refine-research` and `experiment-design`. Use when the user wants a one-shot pipeline from vague research direction to focused final proposal plus detailed experiment roadmap, or asks to build a pipeline, do it end-to-end, or generate both the method and experiment plan together. ([GRIND-Lab-Core/night_owl_research_agent](https://github.com/GRIND-Lab-Core/night_owl_research_agent/tree/HEAD/skills/experiment-design-pipeline) · ★108)
- **experiment-design-pitfalls**: Use when an online controlled experiment (A/B test, split test) is designed, read out or reviewed: one pre-registered hypothesis, one primary metric, guardrails, minimum detectable effect, split and duration fixed before launch, sample size computed from a formula, analysing once instead of peeking, the sample ratio mismatch check, novelty effects, segments and multiple comparisons, results that look too good, negative and inconclusive results, long-term holdouts and whole-product impact. ([techmefr/mentis](https://github.com/techmefr/mentis/tree/HEAD/skills/experiment-design-pitfalls))
- **experiment-design-planner**: Use when the user needs to design, audit, or repair a research experiment, reproduction study, ablation, benchmark, data analysis plan, or pilot study. Focuses on hypotheses, variables, controls, metrics, confounders, failure criteria, reproducibility, and risk tradeoffs. ([YeDonS/codex-research-enhancement-pack](https://github.com/YeDonS/codex-research-enhancement-pack/tree/HEAD/skills/experiment-design-planner))
- **experiment-design-readout**: Use when designing or reading any GTM experiment — an A/B test, a campaign test, a pricing test, a geo holdout or a sales-process trial. Computes the required sample size from the minimum detectable effect before anything runs, refuses to design a test that cannot detect the effect in question, and reads a non-significant result as "we could not detect an effect of this size", never as "it does not work". Prevents peeking, prevents underpowered tests being reported as findings, and prevents a winner declared from mismatched definitions. ([chimeaholu/agentic-gtm-analytics-skillpack](https://github.com/chimeaholu/agentic-gtm-analytics-skillpack/tree/HEAD/analyses/experiment-design-readout) · ★1)
- **experiment-design-readout**: Design an A/B test before it starts and read it honestly after - one primary metric, the minimum effect worth detecting, the sample size and duration that follow, a stopping rule fixed in advance, guardrail metrics, and a readout that reports the interval and refuses to call a winner from peeking or from a sub-group found afterwards. Use when planning an experiment, deciding how long a test must run, or interpreting test results. Not for tests that cannot be randomised. ([modootoday/marketplace](https://github.com/modootoday/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/data-analytics/skills/experiment-design-readout))
- **experiment-design-review**: Review a proposed research experiment for identification, controls, measurements, baseline fairness, feasibility, and stopping criteria. Use before external execution; this Skill never runs code or changes an executor project.
